9: ## Research and execution
10: 
11: The main objectives are a complete expected whole-set witness cost and a nonlocal physical restriction payment. Signed Booleanity, conditional phase entropy, arbitrary weighted thresholds under product biases, and low-energy/shared-variable composition provide distinct routes and substantial class targets. All constants, normalizers, rare-event logarithms and Boolean realizability requirements remain explicit.
12: 
13: The independently audited revision removes targets already proved, adds known counterexamples with their exact scopes, and gives every A-packet a concrete deliverable, acceptance test, bounded initial cycle, intended consumers and independent reviewers. Baseline imports finish once; subsequent runs advance their next unresolved implication. Three stagnant substantive attempts on the same stable gap prompt a change of method or a justified narrower theorem.

16: - [Claims and stable gaps](CLAIM_REGISTRY.json) point to exact source versions and review records.

19: ## Current mathematical frontier
20: 
21: 1. **G-J-COST:** pay the complete conditioned posterior witness expectation, including root normalization, labels, ranks, rarity and latent-witness entropy.
22: 2. **G-R-SELECT / G-R-MISMATCH:** choose actual coordinates/proper blocks or a stopping tree and pay mismatch logarithms minus KL credit plus terminal entropy without reused influence.
23: 3. **G-BOOLEANITY / G-PHASE-COND:** derive a full-Booleanity consequence beyond refuted fixed prefixes, retaining the unpaid conditional spectral entropy.
24: 4. **G-BIASED-LTF:** prove a corrected product-biased weighted-threshold result surviving the three known physical-TC/pivotal-credit controls.
25: 5. **G-LOWSEL-COLLISION:** go beyond the already proved positive-energy selector corollary to a new unbounded family, low-energy collision payment or explicitly controlled reused selectors.

FILE ~\mop_fei100\reports\fei-2026-10-11\FEI_100_agent_research_plan.tex
54: The constant must be independent of dimension, arity, depth, overlap, probability of the minority output, and any unbounded representation parameter. An explicit large constant would settle the conjecture. Optimizing that constant is secondary to proving its finiteness.
55: 
56: Throughout this report, entropy is in bits. Some historical Brain notes use natural logarithms and raw entropy of indicators. A coefficient in such a note cannot be copied into a sign-function statement without the corresponding conversion. Every imported result must state its measure, Fourier basis, normalization, log base, and treatment of the empty coefficient.
57: 
58: \subsection{Recovered manuscripts and verified scope}
59: 
60: The interrupted work supplied a 36-page version of \emph{Fourier entropy of threshold functions and their compositions}. The audited recovery and shared-data extension produce the 38-page public paper, released at repository commit \texttt{cd27aa96a5bb670a5b3ae5451095b7020db8e9d4} \cite{Threshold}. Its principal constant is
61: \[
62:  \Creg=\log_2(4+e)+2\log_2(e\pi)
63:        =8.93647465787758\ldots.
64: \]
65: With $D(f)=\sum_i\min\{\I_i(f),1-\I_i(f)\}$, its Theorem A gives $\HH(f)\le\Creg D(f)$ for every regular Boolean function. Here regular means unate with a total desirability order, and therefore includes every deterministic linear threshold function with arbitrary real weights. It does not mean small or uniformly comparable weights.
66: 
77: The corrected Chakraborty--Kulkarni--Lokam--Saurabh work bounds LTF entropy by $O(\sqrt n)$; an earlier all-LTF claim was removed. The random-LTF result treats specified random models \cite{CKLS,RandomLTF}. This makes verification of the recovered deterministic theorem particularly important. The current argument must stand on its complete proof, independently of earlier abstracts or claims.
78: 
79: Existing coding and composition theorems are essential prerequisites, especially Wan--Wright--Wu and O'Donnell--Tan \cite{WWW,ODT}. Shalev's low-influence reductions explain why rarity alone is not a structural simplification \cite{Shalev}. Recent restriction work supplies a directly relevant stopping-tree framework \cite{GMP}; the plateaued-function theorem supplies a useful structured benchmark \cite{Potapov}. The author's percolation result supplies a geometric encoding example, with spatial hypotheses that must remain explicit \cite{Percolation}.
80: 
81: Do not spend the campaign rediscovering the generic coordinate-marginal entropy estimate as a Boolean breakthrough. Keller--Mossel--Schlank already give the relevant general estimate \cite{KMS}. A later preprint claiming an information-theoretic sharpening was withdrawn because the result already appeared there \cite{Withdrawn}. A new universal argument must use additional structure of actual Boolean spectra or a genuinely stronger global accounting mechanism.
82: 
83: \section{The two highest-priority mathematical endpoints}
84: 
85: \subsection{Endpoint J: bound a complete witness, including its posterior entropy}
300: Fixed prefixes are already known to be insufficient in two
301: distinct relaxations. The archived signed-convolution-order
302: family is normalized and dyadic and passes every prescribed
303: fixed cancellation order while its erasure/influence ratio
304: diverges \cite{BrainBooleanOrder}. A separate cyclic construction
305: passes every fixed atom-cap prefix while retaining an unbounded
306: signed-lifetime ratio \cite{Brain51}. Neither is an actual Boolean
307: FEI counterexample. B07 must state the exact hierarchy and
308: identify an additional consequence of full Booleanity absent
312: \subsection{Arbitrary biased weighted thresholds}\label{sec:weighted-guards}
313: 
314: The public paper supplies the correct product-measure strong entropy normalization and proves it for symmetric thresholds. Extending this to arbitrary weighted thresholds with a constant uniform in weights, biases, and arity would immediately enlarge the depth-independent formula class through the established composition principle \cite{ODT,Threshold}. This is a substantial secondary target even if full FEI remains open.
315: 
316: For clarity, in the orthonormal product Fourier basis with
317: $v_i=4q_i(1-q_i)$ the target normalization is
318: \[
319:  \HH_\mu^+(f)=\sum_{S\ne\varnothing}\wh f_\mu(S)^2
320:        \log_2\frac{\prod_{i\in S}v_i}{\wh f_\mu(S)^2},
321:  \qquad
322:  \Delta_\mu(f)=\I_\mu(f)-\Var_\mu(f).
323: \]
329: 
330: The separate reductions manuscript already supplies three
331: normalization obstructions that every proposed extension must
332: respect \cite{Reductions}. One is the weighted threshold
333: \[
334:  A_m=\left\{mX_0+\sum_{j=1}^mX_j\ge m\right\},\qquad
335:  q=\Pp(X_j=1)=m^{-2},\qquad
336:  q_0=\Pp(X_0=1)=\frac{(m-1)q^m}{1+(m-1)q^m}.
337: \]
370: and formulate a replacement that survives them.
371: 
372: \subsection{Shared-data composition}
373: 
374: For uniform physical selector bits $x\in\bits^a$ disjoint from the data cube and arbitrary Boolean branches $g_b$ on that common data cube, let $F(x,y)=g_x(y)$. With $N=2^a$, set
375: \[
376:  R_T=N^{-1}\sum_b\wh g_b(T)^2,\qquad
377:  r_T(b)=\frac{\wh g_b(T)^2}{NR_T}.
378: \]
379: For $R_T>0$, let $\nu_T$ be the squared normalized Walsh transform of the signed vector $\wh g_b(T)/\sqrt{NR_T}$. Omit zero-mass terms. Then
380: \[
381:  H(F)=a+N^{-1}\sum_bH(g_b)
382:        +\sum_T R_T[H(\nu_T)-H(r_T)].
383: \]
384: This preserves interference exactly. If every branch satisfies
385: $H(g_b)\le C\I(g_b)$, the already proved estimate
415: the data spectral set; $r_T$ is a different law, on physical
416: branch labels. These identities identify the shared unpaid term
417: for B04, B06 and B12, rather than supply three independent
418: payments. Taking $W$ to be every input gives $B_W=\HH(F)$ and is
419: not a reduction in difficulty.
420: 
421: \section{Mandatory obstruction suite and what each item rules out}
422: 
423: Every live universal lemma receives a small relevant subset of this suite before extensive proof work. The suite is a collection of targeted mathematical tests. It is not a demand to rerun every old computation after each edit.
436: Boolean cyclic phase realization & Paying a marginal may force auxiliary energy yet leave conditional entropy. & State and pay the conditional or joint remainder explicitly. \\
437: Coherent near-tensor surgery & A remainder can contain $\alpha^2\log(1/\alpha^2)$. & Prove an amortized logarithmic payment; $O(\alpha^2)$ alone is false. \\
438: Bias endpoints and near dictators & Corrected entropy and $\I-\Var$ can simultaneously degenerate. & Check limiting ratios and normalization credit analytically. \\
439: Bent selector splice & A selected one-bit increment divided by selector influence can diverge. & Do not assert an all-coordinate or selector-only constant; preserve existential scope. \\
440: B05 rare refunds & Fixed local prices on $d,V_{\rm post},M_{\rm inf}$ miss the rare logarithm. & Analyze a chosen complete tree or a nonlocal logarithmic credit. \\
441: Weighted threshold: global TC & Conditional physical $\alpha\operatorname{TC}/\I$ grows with arity. & Prove a replacement normalization that survives Section~\ref{sec:weighted-guards}. \\
442: Weighted threshold: pivotal credit & Coefficient-one pivotal logarithms leave an unbounded residual. & Keep the exact coefficient scope; larger multipliers are not refuted. \\
443: Weighted threshold: local TC & Every coordinate can fail the conditional physical TC payment. & Do not identify this failed target with spectral good-coordinate erasure. \\
444: Fixed convolution or cap prefixes & Distinct non-Boolean families pass every prescribed fixed prefix. & Name the hierarchy and supply an additional full-Booleanity consequence. \\
445: Raw convolution tensors & Raw $L$ can grow exponentially while influence is additive. & Retain normalized $T=H(v/Z)-\log_2Z$ and its still-unpaid bound. \\
446: Overlapping Boolean products & $H(fg)\le H(f)+H(g)$ is false when variables are shared. & Use an exact collision ledger; disjoint additivity remains valid. \\

448: \bottomrule
449: \end{longtable}
450: 
451: The two component examples are exact counterexamples for raw indicator functionals with the units stated in their original records, not unbounded Boolean FEI ratios \cite{Brain44,Brain45}. The coherent-composition and cyclic conditional-scope controls are similarly limited to the statements they actually refute \cite{Brain54}. Source status must travel with the result: an unmerged archive, a finite checker, and a universal analytic proof are different forms of evidence.
452: 
453: 
454: \paragraph{Normalization and retired targets.}
455: For the convolution route, a normalized reference amplitude $b$
456: defines $v_U=(\sum_S b_S b_{S\triangle U})^2$,
457: $Z=\sum_Uv_U$ and $L=\sum_Uv_U\log_2(1/v_U)$.
458: The tensor-additive statistic is
459: \[
460:  T=L/Z=H(v/Z)-\log_2 Z,
461: \]
462: not $H(v/Z)$ alone. The archived genuine Boolean tensor
463: obstruction refutes fixed raw-$L$ payment; it does not refute a
464: correctly normalized $T$ payment \cite{BrainNormalized}.
465: Shared-variable product subadditivity is separately refuted
466: \cite{BrainOverlap}. B08 must retrieve the current exact
467: occupancy-credit definition and the rejected fixed-positive-reserve
468: claim before proposing a replacement. An already disproved
469: helper is retired at its exact quantifiers; the surviving
470: universal endpoint is open until its quantitative estimate is proved.

1272: 
1277: Each run saves \texttt{REPORT.md}, \texttt{CLAIMS.json}, and \texttt{PROGRESS.json}. The progress record names the packet, gap, attempt, mathematical change, proof dependencies, result sources, next consumers, and next action. A claim carries an exact statement and hypothesis hash, version, proof source, contributor list, review records and any supersedes or refutes relationship. A completed artifact, a proved mathematical target, an independent review, a saved mirror and downstream use are separate states. A final honest gap report can complete a handoff while leaving the mathematical target open.
1278: 
1279: When a claim version is refuted, B20 flags its dependent conclusions as \texttt{BLOCKED\_DEPENDENCY} or \texttt{CONDITIONAL}, preserves the original witness, and routes the correction to the named consumers. A consumer records the actual repaired substitution in its next substantive report. Delivery receipts are not evidence that a correction has been used in a proof. The registry organizes evidence; it cannot certify a theorem by changing a status field.
1280: 
1281: At each invocation B20 checks coverage of all hundred A-packets and all report elements: exact endpoints, representation assumptions, normalization, mandatory counterexamples, computations, reviews, class consequences and final handoff. It identifies unattended high-priority work and assigns the nearest ready step within existing capacity. Its own run must also contain a substantive synthesis or derivation. The aim is to finish every useful deliverable while retaining an accurate list of mathematically unresolved targets.
1282: 
1283: \subsection{Review starts immediately}
1284: 
1285: Independent review runs from Day 1. Day 9 is an integrated recheck of the strongest candidates and their repaired dependencies, not the first reading of individual proofs. A stream may hold at most two complete candidate claims awaiting review; when that queue is full, it completes a derivation, resolves an objection, verifies a witness or reviews an independent claim before opening another broad conjecture. Exploratory inferences may continue with explicit conditional status; two complete audits are reserved for full-FEI candidates and major theorems selected for acceptance.
1286: 
1287: A reviewer who supplies a substantive missing proof step becomes a contributor to that version and cannot serve as its independent accepting reviewer. Re-running the author's code or reading the same summary is not an independent reconstruction. A different representation, proof route or source-level reconstruction is required for the critical claim.
1297: The source-pinned supplement at \texttt{research\_pushes/2026-10-11-full-fei-100-agents/} in both private repositories contributes useful mathematics to this same campaign. Its \texttt{NATIVE\_MAPPING.md} explicitly maps advisory F-role groups to the installed B-streams. The F labels do not create another hundred workers or replace the A-packet backlog. Its report, counterexample archive and seed lemmas are consulted for a concrete obligation within the existing assignment.
1298: 
1299: In particular, \texttt{SEED\_LEMMAS.md} already derives a normalized prior mixture of complete reference laws, an effective-piece rank refinement under explicit disjoint-support and fiber-antichain hypotheses, and a point-defect obstruction to every fixed spectral R\'enyi order below one. These are source interfaces, not new unpaid assignments to rederive indefinitely. Their applications still require the entire expected description cost, including the reference normalizer and scale choice. The supplement is pinned at open-problems commit \texttt{bfb1d10b25cb5cb2e09112357cd24b21b5b1fe5e}; its mapping, seed, and campaign files were read directly during reconciliation. Publication of the threshold paper is already complete at public commit \texttt{cd27aa96a5bb670a5b3ae5451095b7020db8e9d4}; a stale upload instruction must not consume future mathematical runs.
1300: 
1301: 
1302: \section{First actions ready to execute}
1303: 
1304: These packets start from the audited baseline and seek a new
1305: quantitative result, rather than reproduce a retired helper:
1319: \item \textbf{B08:} retain the exact mismatch/KL rebate in one
1320: specified tree ledger and formulate a credit for its remaining
1321: logarithmic debt. Preserve normalized convolution and existing
1322: fixed-positive-reserve guards.
1323: \item \textbf{B09:} begin with all three weighted-threshold
1324: normalization failures and state one replacement that survives
1325: them. Prove it on an arity-unbounded class or refute it precisely.
1326: \item \textbf{B07/B16:} identify a full-Booleanity consequence
1327: beyond the two fixed-prefix no-go families. Prove a restricted
1328: conditional phase-energy estimate with the actual conditional
1329: spectral entropy retained and the tensor-embedding guard applied.
1330: \item \textbf{B12:} establish a new unbounded family satisfying
1331: the existing quantitative disagreement corollary, or freeze a
1332: structural collision payment where selector influence per bit
1333: vanishes. Test bent splice before claiming an increment bound.
1334: \item \textbf{B15/B17:} choose one current unproved helper and
1335: freeze an exact bounded countersearch. Certify the logarithmic
1336: comparison for any violation and state the tested domain.

72: 
128: \]

132: ## 3. PROVED: biased uncertainty bound with explicit rare-event tax
133: 
134: ### Theorem B09-BIASED-BLOCK-UNCERTAINTY-01
135: 
136: For every finite Boolean `f`, every interior product-bias vector `p`, and every physical block `A`, equations (0.1)--(0.2) hold.
138: ### Proof
139: 
140: Fix `T subseteq B` with `q_T>0` and define the normalized spectral-fiber vector
141: 
142: \[
143: a_R=\frac{\widehat f_p(R\cup T)}{\sqrt{q_T}},
144: \qquad R\subseteq A.
145: \]
146: 
147: Its squared entries have entropy `H(S_A | S_B=T)`.  Define the normalized physical posterior vector
148: 
149: \[
150: b_z=\frac{\sqrt{\mu_{p_A}(z)}\,\widehat{f_z}_{p_B}(T)}{\sqrt{q_T}},
151: \qquad z\in\{0,1\}^A.
152: \]
153: 
275: Thus the tax-free extension fails by more than `0.1195` bits on the smallest nonlinear genuine Boolean gate.  This is a gate-helper counterexample, not an FEI counterexample: it says nothing about unbounded `H/I`.
276: 
277: ## 5. Composition and prime-gate consequences
278: 
279: O'Donnell--Tan's product-biased composition theorem already reduces disjoint read-once compositions to gate inequalities at the induced output biases.  The exact matrix in (3.1) is compatible with that reduction: it is a tensor product on disjoint blocks, and both `Delta_A` and the aligned `M_A,J_A` ledgers add.
280: 
281: Therefore:
282: 
283: 1. decomposable gates do not create a new violation of (0.1) beyond their factors;
284: 2. the two-bit AND gate proves that a prime-gate proof cannot simply transplant the uniform `J<=M` statement to induced nonuniform biases;
285: 3. a composition proof using this uncertainty route must either pay `Delta_A(p)` once, amortize it against a hierarchy credit, or exploit stronger Boolean phase constraints;
286: 4. because `Delta(p)=p log_2(1/p)+O(p^2)`, replacing it by `O(p)` is impossible at the endpoint scale relevant to rare activation.
287: 
288: The unpaid universal implication remains a dimension-free payment of the actual conditional label cost `M_A` plus the nonreused endpoint tax by the appropriate biased influence/gate potential.
289:

35: ## X004 — Overlapping leaf supports invalidate the mixture identities
36: 
37: **Owners:** F11,F14,F16,F19. **Related:** C029. **Source:** direct two-bit substitution in P, `prop:selector-identities`.
38: 
39: Use cells a_±(z)=(1±z)/2 and balanced leaves g_+=y, g_−=−y. They share the same nonempty Fourier support {y}. The actual selector is F(z,y)=zy, so H(F)=0 and I(F)=2. If one wrongly applies the disjoint-support formula, p_±=1/2, q_± are uniform on the two control subsets, d_±=1/2, and it predicts H=2 and I=3/2.
40: 
41: **Defeats:** the *identities* C010 after removing spectral-support disjointness. Physical selector cells remain disjoint; leaf-variable overlap alone is not the issue. Correct Fourier coefficients must retain cross terms and their signs. This is not a counterexample to an unspecified upper bound with an explicit interference penalty.
42: 
43: ## X005 — Arbitrary signed leaves encode full FEI
44: 
45: **Owners:** F11,F14,F15,F20. **Related:** C014. **Kind:** exact scope reduction, not a numerical counterexample.
47: For singleton address cells A_u={z=u}, choosing constant leaves s_u=g(u) represents any Boolean table: F(z)=sum_u1_{A_u}(z)s_u=g(z). Each leaf has zero entropy and zero influence; cheap individual leaves do not control interference in the resulting address function.
48: 
49: Even if leaves must be balanced, take g_u(y)=g(u)y. Then F(z,y)=g(z)y, H(F)=H(g), I(F)=I(g)+1. All leaves have the same Fourier support. Apply this to the t-fold disjoint tensor power of g and divide by t: a uniform C on this selector class implies H(g)≤CI(g), with the same constant.
50: 
51: For a proposed *strong* bound H(F)≤C(I(F)−1), no limiting argument is needed: I(F)−1=I(g) exactly. See also [REPORT.md](REPORT.md), “Shared leaves can hide the full problem”; this is distinct from the two-cell arbitrary-selector reduction C012.
52: 
53: **Defeats:** declaring “affine/singleton cells and arbitrary signed leaves” an easier general FEI class, or extending the distinct-character/orthogonal-leaf theorem without paying its lost hypothesis. Source: elementary coefficient identity in the setup of P, `sec:selectors`; preserve the explicit distinction between this reduction and C010.
54: 
55: ## X006 — Real coherent amplitudes are not Boolean restrictions
57: **Owners:** F09,F11,F14,F16. **Related:** C033. **Source:** ER §6; Shalev §1.1, https://arxiv.org/html/1806.03646v2 .
58: 
59: The real unit-norm function ψ_n(x)=n^−1/2 sum_i x_i has n Fourier atoms of mass 1/n, H=log₂n and I=1. For growing n it is not Boolean. It cannot be admitted merely because it is a normalized signed superposition of Boolean characters.
60: 
61: **Defeats:** universal entropy/influence bounds that use only normalization and allow arbitrary coherent mixtures. For a true Boolean physical splice the coefficients are (ghat±hhat)/2 on two selector-frequency sectors; neither sector may be independently substituted for a Boolean function. Full signed XOR identities must be checked, not assumed after normalization.
62: 
63: ## X007 — Shared-variable product entropy is not subadditive
64: 
65: **Owners:** F14,F17,F19. **Related:** C030. **Sources:** OP; RM §9.2; corrected primary package linked from OP.
66: 
67: The recovered seven-variable Boolean pair has H(fg)−H(f)−H(g)>0.1630852002095 bits, with a one-point-defect multiplier. The corrected outward interval is [0.16308520020956166,0.16308520020956171]; use the corrected package, not the historical nearest-rounded exports.
69: The explicit rare-clause family also has product-entropy excess 4p²log₂(1/p)+(6/ln2−24)p²+O(p³log(1/p)), while I(f)+I(g)−I(fg)=2p². Thus no fixed multiple of this common-pivotal-overlap deficit repairs subadditivity universally.
70: 
71: **Defeats:** uncharged coherent product composition and fixed linear overlap corrections. Disjoint tensor additivity and averages over physical translations remain valid. The seven-variable table and integer histograms must be loaded from the pinned source before replay; this ledger does not replace that certificate.
72: 
73: ## X008 — Two spectral addresses already need more than constant two
74: 
75: **Owners:** F12,F16,F17. **Related:** C016,C022,C031. **Source:** RM §9.4 and [sources/ONE_BIT_ENDPOINT.md](sources/ONE_BIT_ENDPOINT.md), with original proof and endpoint verification pinned at commit `2591d8acb920ca59230530761efa3113bea2167a`.
76: 
77: The genuine six-variable Boolean witness has two spectral address bits with masses (5/8,1/8,1/8,1/8), and all four conditional retained spectra are product. Its full spectrum has eight atoms of mass 1/16 and thirty-two of mass 1/64; H=5 and influence vector (1/4,1/4,1/2,1/2,1/2,1/8), giving I=17/8 and H/I=40/17>2.
103: For arbitrary nonconstant Boolean f, set g_t=z∏_(j=1)^t f(x^(j)), r_t=ceil(2log₂(t+1)), p_t=2^−r_t, and F_t=1+A_(r_t)(g_t−1). Then I(F_t)→0, P(F_t=−1)→0, and H(F_t)/I(F_t)→H(f)/I(f). The exact formulas are C013.
104: 
105: **Defeats:** claiming that arbitrarily sparse balanced-core activation has a smaller optimal universal FEI constant. A structural restriction on the core may still help; rarity alone does not. Credit the earlier low-influence reductions of Wan–Wright–Wu and Shalev, not just this exact calculation.

107: ## X012 — Addressing calibrates joint label costs
108: 
109: **Owners:** F01–F03,F11,F17,F18. **Related:** C008,C009. **Kind:** exact benchmark, not a counterexample to FEI. **Source:** P, addressing benchmark and `cor:affine-selectors`.
110: 
111: With a control bits and N=2^a data bits, each nonzero Fourier set is T∪{y_u}, and under the spectral law |S|=1+Bin(a,1/2). Thus H=2a and I=1+a/2. The gate label u is paid once per whole set; every set can use one gate, one root, and zero allocation-congestion loss in the recovered queue witness.

145: 
146: Let \(P\) be a probability law on the subsets of a finite set and let
147: \(v=P(\mathcal S\ne\varnothing)\). For every index \(j\) in a finite or countable set, let \(Q_j\) be a subprobability law on the nonempty subsets:
148: 
190: \]
191: 
192: This follows by normalizing \(Q_{\mathrm{mix}}\), applying nonnegativity of KL divergence, and observing that the logarithm of its total mass is nonpositive. If a required reference probability is zero, the bound is simply infinite.
193: 
194: For every positive-weight index,
195: 
196: \[
197: Q_{\mathrm{mix}}(A)\ge w_jQ_j(A)\ge w_j2^{-b_j(A)}.
198: \]
212: \]
213: 
214: Here \(\mathcal C\) is the full cost of a terminating queue witness: root odds, subset sizes and normalizers, subset weights, attachment multisets, child counts, and child-gate labels. The proposition proves that each such witness has probability exactly \(2^{-\Lambda_\lambda-\mathcal C_\lambda}\). Optimizing finite witnesses is valid because they all lower-bound the same reference mass.

364: ## 4. Review and acceptance boundaries
365: 
366: The displayed statements were checked algebraically against the baseline's signed antichain and queue interfaces. Finite computations are not used as a substitute for the proofs. The report audit also independently verified the point-defect asymptotics and the need to retain each normalization term.
367: 
368: An application is a separate claim. It must state which representation is chosen, which ranks and pieces satisfy Section 1, which complete laws and fixed priors satisfy Section 2, and how the entire resulting expectation is paid. 5: All logarithms denoted log_2 are in bits. Write ln for natural logarithms. Let f:{-1,1}^n -> {-1,1}, a_S=E[f chi_S], p_S=a_S^2, H(f)=-sum p_S log_2 p_S and I(f)=sum |S|p_S. Set 0 log 0=0. Let X be the random subset with probability p_S.

8:

10: 
11:     B_i(f)=H(X_i | X_{-i}),   B(f)=sum_i B_i(f),

13: 
14: For each T not containing i let u=a_T, v=a_{T+i}, m=u^2+v^2. The restrictions have coefficients u+v and u-v. Their squared coefficient distributions q_+ and q_- have common average m. In particular m, as a function of T, is a probability distribution.
15:

17: 
18:     B_i(f)=sum_T E(u^2,v^2).

It is strictly positive because the two displayed marginal masses are different and the first conditional law is uniform. Finally h_2(delta)/delta=log_2(1/delta)+1/ln 2+O(delta), proving the asymptotic. QED.

5: ## 1. A norm-arithmetic lemma
6: 
7: Let a be a nonzero finite vector of dyadic rational amplitudes indexed by a Boolean cube. Suppose its normalized squared law is a product law. Then its squared norm w is dyadic and its reduced odd numerator is congruent to 1 modulo 4.
8: 
9: **Proof.** Remove all coordinates deterministic under the product law. On the remaining r active coordinates every amplitude is nonzero. Since ratios of nonzero amplitudes are rational, the ratio of their magnitudes along coordinate i can be written B_i/A_i, where A_i,B_i are positive coprime integers. Product magnitudes then have the form
10: 
11:     |a_s|=c product_i A_i^(1-s_i) B_i^s_i.
12: 
13: The gcd of the integer products is one: for any prime, choose in each coordinate a factor not divisible by that prime. Thus a finite integer combination of those products equals one. Since all |a_s| are dyadic, the same combination shows that c is dyadic.
25: Indeed, write wj=uj/2^dj in reduced form. Both dj>=1. Unequal denominators give an odd numerator when brought to the larger denominator, which cannot equal its even denominator. Thus d0=d1=d. Since u0 and u1 are each 1 modulo 4, their sum is 2 modulo 4; hence 2^d has d=1, forcing u0=u1=1.
26: 
27: If a dyadic product-amplitude vector has norm squared 1/2, every nondegenerate marginal of its normalized squared law is 1/2. In the factorization above the odd part of w is one, so each primitive A_i²+B_i² is a power of two. This forces A_i=B_i=1. The number r of active fair coordinates is odd, since c²2^r=1/2 and c is dyadic. At norm squared one the same argument gives an even number of fair coordinates, recovering the prior exact-product endpoint.
28: 
29: ## 2. One address bit is paid with constant two
30: 
31: ### Theorem 2.1
32: 
33: Let f be Boolean, and suppose that, conditional on either positive-probability value of one spectral coordinate X_j, the squared Fourier law of all other coordinates is a product law. Then
58:     [-4,-4,-4,4,-4,4,4,4,2,2,2,2,2,2,2,2].
59: 
60: The normalized squared law is product with marginals `(1/2,1/2,1/2,1/5)`, and its squared norm is 5/8. This is allowed by the norm-arithmetic lemma: its odd numerator is five.
61: 
62: Let h1,h2,h3 be the indicators of the three disjoint edges specified by low-three-bit values 3,5,6 respectively, with bit3 free. These edges partition the zero set of g. Introduce two address bits y1,y2 and put
63: 
64:     F(y1,y2,x)=g(x)+chi_1(y)h1(x)+chi_2(y)h2(x)
65:                        +chi_{1,2}(y)h3(x).
66: 
71:     (5/8,1/8,1/8,1/8).
72: 
73: Every normalized conditional spectrum is product. The first has the marginals above, and the other three have `(1/2,1/2,1/2,0)`.
74: 
75: The full squared Fourier law has eight atoms of mass 1/16 and thirty-two atoms of mass 1/64. Hence H(F)=5. Directly from the four projection laws, or from pivotal-edge counting, its physical influence vector in order `(y1,y2,x0,x1,x2,x3)` is
76: 
77:     (1/4,1/4,1/2,1/2,1/2,1/8).

85: - T=H(v/Z)-log Z, **not** H(v/Z); T is exactly additive on amplitude tensors
86: - **REFUTED:** universal K>=theta L-kappa I for raw L and any fixed theta>0, finite kappa. The genuine Boolean three-bit one-point factor has Z=319/256>1, so tensor powers give exponential L and linear K,I
87: - **REFUTED:** universal recovery K_shape<=A T_conditioned+B I for fixed A,B. Paired-coordinate one-point lifts have T_conditioned/I->0, K_shape/I=m-3+o(1), and K_supp/I->1/ln 2. This under-recovery result does **not** refute K_shape>=T/4-kappa I
88: - **PROVEN restricted repair:** unconditioned Q with the specified + sign completion at zeros on that family gives T_completed/4-K_shape=o(I)
89: - For any Boolean source with spectral zeros in dimension >=2, one physical-point flip fills every zero and preserves every existing Fourier sign. This realizes the completion signs in the explicit paired family; reference amplitudes are not thereby Boolean Fourier coefficients, and the original marginals remain the reference
90: 
91: [Complete tensor, support-loss, and surgery proofs](https://github.com/yspennstate/math-open-problems/blob/fe94de760ee2b47545419a7447b492816bd16045/research_pushes/2026-09-30-fei-normalized-convolution-0236z/REPORT.md). “+ at every zero” is not a globally tensor-compatible rule; tensor identities for completed references require product completions.
92: 
93: **CONDITIONAL full-FEI admission, not a proved premise.** Even only for full-support Boolean f with I(f)<=1/16, a universal
94: 
95:     K_full >= T/4-kappa I
96: 
97: would imply **full FEI** with constant kappa+2+log e. Full support removes completion ambiguity. The reduction tensors an arbitrary seed, fills zeros with one-point surgery, and sparsely activates it. [Complete admission proof](https://github.com/yspennstate/math-open-problems/blob/fe94de760ee2b47545419a7447b492816bd16045/research_pushes/2026-09-30-fei-log-allocation/COLLISION_ADMISSION.md). Thus small influence and full support do not make this a solved or demonstrably easier problem. The raw-L falsifier does not refute normalized T. For a broader collision construction, separately prove both valid lower payment and sufficient recovery of the required deficit; these are different tests.

165:

In the specified quantum-inspired degree split, the rounded influence grows at least as a constant times `sqrt(d) 3^(d/4)` asymptotically. Independence, flatness and disjoint controls are essential. No entropy upper bound for the entire rounded family, obstruction to every classicalization, or classical FEI theorem follows.
177: This is an averaged conditional-TC bound, not a claim for every branch or a physical restriction algorithm. On an actual ratio-critical Boolean source, the prior criticality ledger gives `DTC<=c0 I`, `c0=2/ln 2`, so the address costs at most `c0 I/eta`. The optional all-block influence estimate requires ratio maximality over **all physical descendants**, not only immediate children; the [analytic review](https://github.com/yspennstate/math-open-problems/blob/0add87669946788d041f3ee5e2f27ec10316c90b/proofnet24/P03-fei/continuations/2026-09-30-entropy-wringing/AUDIT.md) records this correction.
178: 
179: For partial Walsh projections `g_T(x)=E_y f(y,x)chi_T(y)`, weights `w_T=||g_T||_2^2`, and normalized squared laws `q_T`, full Booleanity retains the **pointwise all-shift** identities
180: 
181:     sum_T g_T(x)g_(T xor U)(x)=1_(U empty).
182: 
183: The explicitly sufficient collective target remains **OPEN**:
184: 
185:     sum_T w_T H(q_T)
188: for universal finite `K,L`. The source proves that such a statement would imply full FEI. It does not prove the statement or show it easier than FEI.
189: 
190: **REFUTED component replacement.** The [component obstruction](https://github.com/yspennstate/math-open-problems/blob/0add87669946788d041f3ee5e2f27ec10316c90b/proofnet24/P03-fei/continuations/2026-09-30-entropy-wringing/COMPONENT_OBSTRUCTION.md) constructs bounded, exactly dyadic, actual Boolean partial means with exactly product normalized spectra for which
191: 
192:     H(q)/[I(g)/||g||_2^2 + log(1/||g||_2^2)] -> infinity.
193: 
194: Adding a multiple of `TC(q)=0` does not help. The other projections and the full address entropy remain uncharged; this is neither a counterexample to the collective target nor to FEI. Product rigidity for genuine Boolean functions cannot be applied separately to these multivalued projections.
195: 
196: **Physical-transfer guardrail.** The exact Boolean locking transform `L_g(z,t,x)=(-1)^(zt)g(x xor t*1)` has `H=H(g)+2`, `I=I(g)+1`, and unchanged DTC, but one physical z-query increases expected DTC by `B(g)` while `Inf_z=1/2`. It refutes an unconditional O(query-influence) DTC drift bound. That query is already affordable, with `Delta_z H/Inf_z=4`; critical-only or stop-on-certified-function rules are not refuted. The clipped-potential obstruction is only for slopes admitted by the source's explicit seed criterion, not arbitrary slopes such as 100.
224: ### 9.7 Low-influence reduction credit and reuse boundary
225: 
226: The [low-influence source note](https://github.com/yspennstate/math-open-problems/blob/ecc6da6b0c2514fd885bd2c4de03eb6f26f13f3b/research_pushes/2026-09-30-fei-log-allocation/LOW_INFLUENCE_SOURCE_NOTE.md) credits Shalev, arXiv:1806.03646v2, Theorem 3 and Section 3.2: a universal FEI theorem on `I(f)<=2^(-s(n))`, `s(n)=o(n)`, already implies full FEI; the activation construction is attributed there to Wan–Wright–Wu. Thus neither the general low-influence hardness principle nor tensor-plus-activation is new here. Section 4's particular normalized-collision premise, constants, and exact full-support perturbation remain unchanged, with the premise still unproved.
227: 
228: **Before a new attempt:** retain the actual signed joint Boolean constraints, distinguish a spectral address from a physical query, apply the strongest prior product/mismatch results at their valid scope, use the final dual-semantics exports, and state the new missing implication. Independent analytic review, exact finite arithmetic, floating regression, formal verification, remote delivery, merge state and downstream use remain separate evidence tiers. All research-source links in this addendum are immutable; PR links report delivery only. Publication priority remains unverified throughout.

232: ### 11.13 Direct trajectory budget and the actual biased-Boolean remainder
242: **Retain the history refund.** [The exact history note](https://github.com/yspennstate/math-open-problems/blob/c63180b23321ce9130dbcbab825546f6c14923d0/proofnet24/P03-fei/continuations/2026-09-30-entropy-wringing/scalar-information-remainder/integrated-trajectory/BOOLEAN_HISTORY_REFUND.md) expresses C as the pivotal Jensen envelope minus conditional information in the actual record. On AND_n the envelope grows like nI while the true cost is O(I). The prephysical record refund integrates to TC(P)+an error in[0,I], but the full refund also contains actual K minus the no-history physical information Kbar. Kbar itself can be order nI and cannot be dropped. The AND leading constants and their integrable domination are recorded; this is an overcharge guard, not a new FEI bound.
243: 
244: **Stronger source structure, not independent sphere rows.** In [the biased-Boolean structure note](https://github.com/yspennstate/math-open-problems/blob/c63180b23321ce9130dbcbab825546f6c14923d0/proofnet24/P03-fei/continuations/2026-09-30-entropy-wringing/scalar-information-remainder/integrated-trajectory/BIASED_BOOLEAN_STRUCTURE.md), the fixed-time clicked set A is sufficient; detailed click times are ancillary given A. With theta=1−exp(−t), its unnormalized amplitude b_A(x) is exactly the biased Fourier coefficient of the actual Boolean function eta↦f(x eta), where E eta_i=sqrt(1−theta). Thus mu_theta^x(A)=b_A(x)² obeys the complete weighted biased product identities, and all x-rows are translates of one f. The exact C functional uses adjacent coefficients, or equivalently the biased transforms of actual complementary ternary pairs.
245: 
246: A proved sufficient reduction retains this structure:
247: 
248:     H_nat(f)<=(4+2ln2)I(f)+Q_bias(f),
249:     Q_bias=integral_0^1 E_x[sum_i H_mu_theta^x(A_i|A_-i)] dtheta/theta.
250: 
251: The integral is still unpaid. Its integrand is the full conditional-entropy sum of the actual biased-Boolean law, not TC or the unconditional thinning marginal. The independently checked reverse inequality now gives |Q_bias−C_path|<=(1+2ln2)I, hence H_nat−(4+2ln2)I<=Q_bias<=H_nat+(2+2ln2)I. Thus this is an FEI-equivalent bank, not an oversized proxy. Characters give Q_bias=(pi²/6)I as a normalization check. This does not establish the retired universal pointwise erasure bound.

279: ### 11.15 Pointwise restoration limits and explicit source-aware criteria
281: **Pointwise Hellinger transfer needs more than the paid reserve.** The [real-state bent/spike guard](https://github.com/yspennstate/math-open-problems/blob/b1b9d08716766c3ef373de8350867ccae5314755/proofnet24/P03-fei/continuations/2026-09-30-entropy-wringing/scalar-information-remainder/integrated-trajectory/adaptive-minority/HELLINGER_TRANSFER_OBSTRUCTION.md) has common marginal energy J, bounded TC and a same-sign marginal-product reference, but C(h)−C(reference) is order J while sqrt(J TC)+TC is only order sqrt(J). Thus no fixed coefficient on that error gives a universal pointwise comparison, even with a fixed multiplier on the reference cost. An additive C J term is not refuted. The [separately reviewed optional embedding](https://github.com/yspennstate/math-open-problems/blob/b1b9d08716766c3ef373de8350867ccae5314755/proofnet24/P03-fei/continuations/2026-09-30-entropy-wringing/scalar-information-remainder/integrated-trajectory/adaptive-minority/OPTIONAL_HISTORY_EMBEDDING.md) realizes the same failure on an actual all-one no-click history of the finite-mesh minority policy. A dyadic completion near Maj17 and address parity inflation keep every selected marginal below one-half. Its original source H/I tends to zero, so neither high-ratio criticality nor integrated source-influence payment is contradicted.
282: 
283: **A positive inverse theorem keeps its actual prices.** The [source-aware inverse note](https://github.com/yspennstate/math-open-problems/blob/b1b9d08716766c3ef373de8350867ccae5314755/proofnet24/P03-fei/continuations/2026-09-30-entropy-wringing/scalar-information-remainder/integrated-trajectory/adaptive-minority/inverse-noise/INVERSE_NOISE_GUARD.md) proves normalized recovery error at most the undamped source tail plus kappa² TC, with kappa the exact inverse condition number. Degree-weighted recovery additionally retains the source energy tail. Grid rounding loses degree, and exact low-degree decoding restores the actual source without proving product structure. The [arithmetic criterion](https://github.com/yspennstate/math-open-problems/blob/b1b9d08716766c3ef373de8350867ccae5314755/proofnet24/P03-fei/continuations/2026-09-30-entropy-wringing/scalar-information-remainder/integrated-trajectory/adaptive-minority/inverse-noise/PRODUCT_RIGIDITY.md) supplies one severe sufficient repair: a nonproduct probability law with atoms on a 1/M grid is at TV distance at least1/(3M²) from every product law. Full inverse error below1/(36M^4) therefore forces the actual low-degree grid source's spectrum to be exactly product. This threshold explicitly requires inverse conditioning and the full inverse-reference tail; the integrated TC budget does not supply them.
284: 
285: **Actual Boolean controls defeat an unpriced shortcut.** For the four-bit one-point flip, every ordinary degree truncation of the full inverse product reference rounds to the constant+1 at sufficiently late no-click times, with squared source error1/4 despite TC tending to zero. Its late branch has probability at least49/64; unweighted source-reconstruction error has infinite time integral. This is distinct from the current-amplitude error. The [dimension-growing point-flip control](https://github.com/yspennstate/math-open-problems/blob/b1b9d08716766c3ef373de8350867ccae5314755/proofnet24/P03-fei/continuations/2026-09-30-entropy-wringing/scalar-information-remainder/integrated-trajectory/adaptive-minority/inverse-noise/POINT_FLIP_SURPLUS.md), with r=n^−3, has TC/J tending to zero but H/J−ln(1/r) growing as2n ln2. It refutes adding only sqrt(J TC)+TC to the common-noise log-time bound for arbitrary approximately product Boolean ancestors. The sources are actual and their paths follow minority selection, but adjusted criticality is not claimed.
286: 
287: **Approximate Booleanity and positivity are still insufficient.** The [compact positive-source guard](https://github.com/yspennstate/math-open-problems/blob/b1b9d08716766c3ef373de8350867ccae5314755/proofnet24/P03-fei/continuations/2026-09-30-entropy-wringing/scalar-information-remainder/integrated-trajectory/adaptive-minority/critical-source-guard/POSITIVE_PROXY_GUARD.md) reuses the earlier random-sign product component to build one positive, uniformly near-Boolean real source with DTC=o(I), squared full-convolution defect=o(I), and the paid adaptive K/correlation reserves, yet C_integral/I diverges. It is not Boolean. The [critical-source interface](https://github.com/yspennstate/math-open-problems/blob/b1b9d08716766c3ef373de8350867ccae5314755/proofnet24/P03-fei/continuations/2026-09-30-entropy-wringing/scalar-information-remainder/integrated-trajectory/adaptive-minority/critical-source-guard/CRITICAL_SOURCE_INTERFACE.md) retains the exact indicator block ledger, its immediate consequence q_i<=exp(3−rho), and an alternative proof of the already known indicator-product classification. These are scope controls and recovered consequences, not a new constructive payment.
288: 
289: Root, peer and maintainer analytic reviews are recorded with the precise source hypotheses. The maintainer's isolated replays matched all final outputs byte-for-byte: seven rational-log bent/spike cases; thirteen four-bit noise cases; forty-two compressed point-flip dimensions;27,540 rational-law comparisons and32,640 Boolean spacing pairs; and the exact65,536-atom positive-source fixture. Numerical entropy columns remain diagnostic where labeled. All23 files changed in this final batch were exactly read back at draft PR12 head b1b9d08716766c3ef373de8350867ccae5314755; the local manifest audit found176 entries/176 unique files and zero mismatches. The draft remains unmerged. Full FEI, actual critical-source entropy restoration and the equivalent C/Q_bias payment remain open.
295:     H_nat(f)<=C[I(f)ln(1+I(f))+I(f)+TC(P_f)],
296: 
297: for a universal constant inherited from the published theorem. When I<=1, this becomes H<=C'(I+TC). This is a real stopping endpoint if an actual Boolean source with TC=O(I) can be produced. It does not apply the imported Boolean theorem to multivalued projections or trajectory fields. The primary statement and normalization were independently read; the rational checker certifies only the elementary moment constants. The recovered September23 W3-04 KKLMS barrier/reference-charge report is explicitly credited without a novelty claim.
298: 
299: The [full-trajectory density note](https://github.com/yspennstate/math-open-problems/blob/973b5214bae8803f3351fba42b130757b348cbda/proofnet24/P03-fei/continuations/2026-09-30-entropy-wringing/scalar-information-remainder/integrated-trajectory/integrated-density/REPORT.md) preserves a different positive fact. For bounded real g with M=||g||infinity and g_s=T_sqrt(s)g,
300: 
301:     integral_0^1 ||g_s||²[H(P_s)+TC(P_s)] ds/s
302:       <=2I(g)+Var(g)−Ent(g²)+4ln2 M TV(g).
303: 
340: Thus J<=1 gives a dimension-free ternary-source endpoint. For scaled-ternary g=a u, retain the norm surcharge ln(a²/||g||²). Heat-smoothed fields and general many-bit Fourier projections are not thereby ternary.
341: 
342: The [complete independent dependency audit](https://github.com/yspennstate/math-open-problems/blob/84b0d71fee51f9f514cf65cedada10be391c90e2/proofnet24/P03-fei/continuations/2026-09-30-entropy-wringing/scalar-information-remainder/integrated-trajectory/boolean-product-endpoint/TERNARY_DEPENDENCY_AUDIT.md) checks every Boolean-specific source use through primary Sections2–6 and7.1. The intermediate half-integer alphabet is uniformly bounded and closed under restrictions and parity substitutions; its first half-differences have minimum nonzero magnitude1/4. It is used only for the positive-degree two-function estimate. A nearest-integer centering and quantized Gross bound replace the indicator variance argument, while the empty atom is paid only after returning to original ternary u. The constant intermediate source1/2 still refutes any unqualified energy-only raw-entropy claim.

Root read the complete corrected adaptation and dependency audit and checked their arguments; root is not represented as having independently reread every primary dependency. Both root and maintainer isolated elementary replays byte-matched25 alphabet edge pairs and6,654 ternary truth tables. These check only the stated elementary claims, not the multiscale proof. All six additive/updated package files were exactly read back at unmerged draft PR12 head 84b0d71fee51f9f514cf65cedada10be391c90e2. No arbitrary-integer, filtered-state, approximation-stability, novelty or full-FEI claim is made.

348: The [separate decaying-threshold policy](https://github.com/yspennstate/math-open-problems/blob/b052f7ac40a2aef7e4768aa4f0d9836d6906b939/proofnet24/P03-fei/continuations/2026-09-30-entropy-wringing/scalar-information-remainder/integrated-trajectory/forced-reveal/DECAYING_THRESHOLD.md) keeps the same fixed outcome-one clocks, but fully reveals/removes a coordinate when q_i>=epsilon exp(−2t). It counts forced zeros and uses the one original time axis. Cascades are finite. Common forward-noise ancestry survives; the old unweighted removal-count bound and unconditional physical flatness are not asserted for this policy.

1:      FOURIER ENTROPY OF THRESHOLD FUNCTIONS AND THEIR
2:                                               COMPOSITIONS
3: 
4:                                                         YITZCHAK SHMALO
5: 
6:      Abstract. We prove the Fourier entropy­influence inequality for every linear threshold function,
7:      without restrictions on its weights or threshold. The argument applies to regular Boolean functions
8:      and gives an O(log(m + 1)) bound for arbitrary Boolean combinations of m such functions. These
10:      minus variance. We also obtain an O(log(k + 1)) bound for unions of regular functions in which
11:      each variable occurs at most k times. For symmetric thresholds under arbitrary product measures,
12:      we prove the strengthened entropy inequality needed for composition. Consequently, read-once
13:      formulas of arbitrary depth and fan-in over symmetric threshold and parity gates satisfy FEI. The
14:      proofs use antichain estimates for pivotal fibers, spectral reference measures, and product-measure
15:      composition.

1984: 8.2. The product-measure question for weighted thresholds. The next composition question
1985: is whether an absolute constant C satisfies
1986: 
1987:                                               Hµ+(f )  C Iµ(f ) - Varµ(f )
1988: 
1989: for every weighted linear threshold function and every product measure. Theorem 6.6 proves this for
1990: symmetric thresholds. A positive answer for arbitrary weights would remove the balance assumption
1991: on weighted internal gates in Corollary 7.4, by the same exact composition identities.
1992: 
1993:    The antichain argument raises a second question. Can a spectral reference measure control the
1994: cost of many incomparable coordinate orders without a factor depending on their number? The
1995: width bound of Theorem 4.2 and the overlap bound of Theorem C provide two ways to limit that
1996: cost. The unrestricted FEI conjecture requires an argument without either restriction.

1:      FOURIER ENTROPY OF THRESHOLD FUNCTIONS AND THEIR
2:                                               COMPOSITIONS
3: 
4:                                                         YITZCHAK SHMALO
5: 
6:      Abstract. We prove the Fourier entropy­influence inequality for every linear threshold function,
7:      with a constant below 8.937 and no restrictions on its weights or threshold. The argument applies
8:      to regular Boolean functions and gives logarithmic bounds for their Boolean combinations and for
9:      unions with bounded variable overlap. Character multiplication preserves the estimates and yields
10:      bounds by influence minus variance. Under arbitrary product measures, symmetric thresholds satisfy
11:      the strengthened inequality needed for read-once composition, uniformly in depth and fan-in. A
12:      joint description of each Fourier set refines the overlap estimate and recovers constant bounds for
13:      addressing. Exact selector identities extend the bound to orthogonal multiplexers with shared data.
14:      Rare activation preserves the optimal constant of unrestricted FEI. The proofs combine pivotal
15:      antichains, spectral reference measures, and product-measure composition.
16:

216:    O'Donnell and Tan [17] introduced the product-measure strengthening of FEI used in composition
217: and proved its closure under disjoint substitution. They obtained FEI for read-once formulas over
218: any fixed finite gate basis. Our formula results use their composition principle; the additional input
219: is a bound uniform in the arity of symmetric threshold gates, together with the threshold and
220: regular-gate bounds above.

1335: 5.8. Scope of the refinement. The variational inequality supplies a reference measure and an
1336: exact cost for each finite witness. To obtain full FEI from it, one would still need a uniform influence
1337: bound for the expected joint witness cost, including its rank, sparse-root and normalization terms.
1338: The addressing estimate proves such a bound for that family. It does not give one for arbitrary
1339: Boolean functions. The two-cell selector reduction in Section 8 shows that even unrestricted selector
1340: gates already retain the full FEI problem.

2701: 9.2. The product-measure question for weighted thresholds. The next composition question
2702: is whether an absolute constant C satisfies
2703: 
2704:                                               H+µ (f )  C Iµ(f ) - Varµ(f )
2705: 
2706: for every weighted linear threshold function and every product measure. Theorem 6.6 proves this for
2707: symmetric thresholds. A positive answer for arbitrary weights would remove the balance assumption
2708: on weighted internal gates in Corollary 7.4, by the same exact composition identities.
2709: 
2710:    The antichain argument raises a second question. Can a spectral reference measure control the
2711: cost of many incomparable coordinate orders without a factor depending on their number? The
2712: width bound of Theorem 4.2 and the overlap bound of Theorem C provide two ways to limit that
2713: cost. The unrestricted FEI conjecture requires an argument without either restriction.
